| EXP-0 (OD-X1, OD-X2, OD-X7, D1-D7 of the exam plan) | Exam packs plan 2026-10-17 (owner) | **Owner decisions on exam-pack sourcing.** DECIDED 2026-10-17: (D1) all sources allowed: scraping (super admin, manual VPS script), upload by super admin and organisation admin, AI-written questions, licensed content; every item records its source and licence status. (D2) exact questions with minor synonym changes are acceptable (mode A2 'light edit', rights `derived_from_source`, source reference kept, one-click takedown). (D3) keep clone-per-tenant, decide again at B2C (EXP-14). (D4) follow the recommendation; when the source gives a difficulty, map it to the 1 to 5 scale and keep the raw label. (D5) English plus Hindi in Wave 1. (D6) super admin and admin review every question and answer before students are invited (review gate EXP-18). (D7) no permission letters to the exam bodies; proceed as in D2. STILL OPEN: the owner verifies the SSC CGL Tier 1 blueprint against the official notice (the research could not open official sites); a lawyer review of plan § 9 stays optional. | Purpose: lock the rules before schema work. Why recorded in full: D1 and D2 reverse the 2026-10-09 decisions OD-X1 (platform-authored only) and OD-X2 (original content, no verbatim papers). The research found that exam papers are copyrightable works, that aggregator sites ban crawlers, and that a lightly edited copy is still a copy in law; the exposure is the owner's decision and is logged here. Safeguards kept: every item has `rights_status` and a source reference; `withdrawn` removes an item from every pack at the next sync while attempts keep their frozen version; organisation uploads carry a content warranty; scraped items stay in staging until reviewed. Considered and rejected: building verbatim ingestion with no provenance (a takedown could not be honoured). Not included: legal advice. Downstream: EXP-2 (rights values), EXP-7 (mode A2), EXP-16, EXP-17, EXP-18, D3 feeds EXP-14. Full context: `docs/design/2026-10-17-exam-pack-source-and-taxonomy-plan.md` § 0. | ✅ closed 2026-10-19 (S30, owner Q49): EXP-0: the exam-pack rules stand. Why a hard gate: with scraped and lightly edited items, a wrong key is likely, and a candidate who sees a wrong key loses trust. What exists: question status draft, active, archived, ai_draft; no review state; assessments freeze their pool at publish (`assessment_frozen_pool`, 0096). Not included: a separate reviewer role (decommissioned 2026-10-03, so the admin and super admin do it), two-person review, automatic key checking. Downstream: modules 04, 05, 10, 14; the publish and invite routes in module 05 (load-bearing path: Opus review plus Sonnet takeover gate), docs/03, help ids, bulk 'verify all in this pack' with a count and a confirm. | ✅ part 2 built (S22, 2026-10-18, `0ed17887`; part 1 `e5f5e5c` earlier; gaps are row Z-52): module 04 `service/review.ts` and `review-routes.ts`, page `/admin/question-bank/:id/review`, 6 help ids, no migration. Invitations AND `startAttempt` refuse `in_review`, `disputed` and `needs_fix` questions with `QUESTIONS_NOT_VERIFIED`; publish takes `FOR SHARE` and re-checks inside the freeze transaction; editing a verified question resets it to `in_review`; imports start `in_review`; Verify / Needs fix on an archived question is refused with 409 `QUESTION_ARCHIVED`. Gate verdict REVISE, then 3 fixes applied. Owner decision: no backfill of NULL review state. Earlier status: PARTIAL (2026-10-18): the publish gate shipped in module 05 (`QUESTIONS_NOT_VERIFIED`, `service/lifecycle.ts`, test `publish-review-gate.test.ts`); NULL `review_state` passes by design. STILL OPEN: Verify / Needs fix / Withdraw actions and review screen, per-verification audit row, bulk verify with confirm, import and clone setting `in_review`, help ids. Owner decision needed when those land: should NULL also block? Gate detail (`e5f5e5c`, 2026-10-18): publish is blocked while the pool holds a question in `in_review`, `disputed` or `needs_fix`; a NULL `review_state` passes. OWNER QUESTION: should NULL also block? If yes, every new tenant-authored question is blocked until a verify action exists (part 2). RACE NOTE: the pool check and the freeze are separate statements; add `FOR SHARE` or re-check the pool inside the freeze when part 2 adds verify actions. | `05` publish route; `assessment_frozen_pool` (0096) | Claude |

Proposed defaults for the owner to confirm: (1) certificates and the public verification page only for real exams; (2) mock results are labelled 'Mock' for the candidate and the admin and are kept out of the real-exam reports and cohort numbers; (3) a mock does not use up the attempts allowance of the real exam (Z-39 max attempts); (4) the same candidate may be invited to both modes and counts once toward the Free 5-candidate cap; (5) AI-graded questions in a mock count toward the 5 AI attempts on Free, so a mock uses auto-scored items by default; (6) an assessment keeps one default mode that the admin can override per invitation batch. Monitoring comes in tiers (rules only for practice, rules plus human review of flagged events for certifications, live human only for the highest stakes). SUGGESTED DESIGN, owner to confirm: one choice 'Test type' with presets. MOCK = any device, no camera, feedback and explanations allowed, labelled 'Mock', no certificate, kept out of real reports. REAL EXAM = laptop or desktop only, camera on (photos 90 days, video 7 days), no feedback during the test, certificates and reports count. REAL EXAM AT A SCHOOL = real exam with the camera off unless the school admin turns it on.
